I · Agreement between organizations

Introduction

These Terms and Conditions set out the general conditions applicable to the engagement, participation and collaboration of the organizations and entities that carry out activities under the Project “Building Bridges, Weaving Paths: Promoting Actions for the Enforcement of Rights and the International Protection of LGBTIQ+ People in Situations of Human Mobility in Ibero-America” (hereinafter, the Project), led by Corporación Caribe Afirmativo.

Any organization or entity that expresses its acceptance of these Terms and Conditions through the means provided for that purpose shall acquire, for the purposes of this instrument, the status of “Participating Organization” and shall be subject to the conditions, obligations and responsibilities established here, subject to the following recitals.

Recitals

  1. Caribe Afirmativo is a not-for-profit entity that carries out actions aimed at the promotion, recognition, protection and enforcement of human rights, particularly those of LGBTIQ+ people and of those in situations of particular vulnerability.
  2. Caribe Afirmativo runs the project “Building Bridges, Weaving Paths: Promoting actions for the enforcement of rights and the international protection of LGBTIQ+ people in situations of human mobility in Ibero-America” (hereinafter, the “Project”), under which actions of coordination and collaboration are promoted with organizations and entities that carry out activities related to its objectives.
  3. That the implementation of the Project requires coordination between Caribe Afirmativo and participating organizations or entities located in different territories, which may carry out support, profiling, information management and other activities necessary to meet its objectives, in accordance with the functions corresponding to each of them.
  4. That, for the development of the activities associated with the Project, platforms, information systems, applications, infrastructure, electronic means or other technological solutions may be used to facilitate the collection, recording, organization, consultation, storage, exchange, circulation, analysis and management of information. The use of such means shall be understood on the basis of technological neutrality, so that the implementation and term of this Agreement shall not depend on any specific technology, platform, provider, infrastructure, application or information system.
  5. That the participation of the organizations in the Project is collaborative in nature and does not pursue any profit-making purpose, but rather the fulfilment of its legitimate objectives and, where appropriate, the carrying out of activities for historical, statistical, scientific, research, profiling and knowledge-generation purposes, in compliance with the applicable legal safeguards.
  6. That, in connection with the activities carried out under the Project, it may be necessary to collect, supply, consult, use, store, exchange, transmit or carry out other operations on information, including personal data and data that by their nature are subject to special protection conditions.
  7. The Parties acknowledge the particular importance of guaranteeing the privacy, confidentiality, integrity and security of the information used in the course of the Project, as well as the protection of the rights of its data subjects, particularly where sensitive information or people in vulnerable situations are involved.
  8. That each participating organization retains its legal, administrative, technical and financial autonomy and carries out the activities corresponding to it under its own responsibility, in compliance with the obligations assumed under this Agreement and with the legal and regulatory provisions applicable to it.
  9. That the conclusion and performance of this Agreement does not constitute a partnership, mandate, agency, representation, employment relationship, business association or any other form of legal integration between Caribe Afirmativo and the Participating Organization other than the obligations expressly assumed under these Terms and Conditions.
  10. That acceptance of this Agreement does not in itself entail any assignment, transfer or disposal of the intellectual property rights of Caribe Afirmativo or of the Participating Organization, which shall remain with their respective holders, unless expressly agreed otherwise.
  11. That organizations may take part in the Project from different countries and territories, which makes it necessary to establish general and uniform conditions of engagement, without prejudice to compliance with the mandatory provisions applicable to each Participating Organization by reason of its jurisdiction and of the activities it carries out.
  12. That the offer, acceptance, accession and other expressions of will relating to this Agreement may be transmitted by means of electronic equipment, systems and media, and shall produce the corresponding legal effects where they make it possible to establish the Participating Organization's expression of will and to retain evidence of it.
  13. That, regardless of the territory from which electronic acceptance is transmitted, the Parties agree that this instrument shall be deemed concluded and signed, for all contractual purposes, in the city of Barranquilla, Atlántico, Republic of Colombia, without prejudice to any applicable mandatory rules.
  14. That, in view of the international nature of the collaboration and of the information that may be managed during the Project, it is necessary to establish rules on the obligations of the Parties, confidentiality, information security, liability, duration, termination, effects of termination, dispute resolution and personal data protection.

In view of the foregoing, the Participating Organization, by accepting these Terms and Conditions, expresses its will to accede to the Framework Memorandum of Understanding and to be bound by the following clauses.

1Subject matter

This Agreement establishes the conditions governing the collaboration between Caribe Afirmativo and the organizations or entities taking part in the project “Building Bridges, Weaving Paths: promoting actions for the enforcement of rights and the international protection of LGBTIQ+ people in situations of human mobility in Ibero-America” (the “Project”).

Caribe Afirmativo includes human mobility among its lines of work, aimed at transforming the social, institutional and policy frameworks that regulate, interpret and respond to the realities of LGBTIQ+ people in contexts of forced displacement and voluntary migration, whether internal or international. Accordingly, the purpose of the Project is, through the proper management of information, to help improve the support provided to this population, to produce studies and practical guides, and to formulate recommendations that respond, from a cross-cutting perspective, to their particular needs in contexts of human mobility.

Partner Organizations shall facilitate the voluntary provision of information by participating LGBTIQ+ people, through the forms and other technological tools that Caribe Afirmativo makes available, now or in the future, for the development of the Project (the “Project Tools”). To that end, they must inform data subjects in advance about the processing of their personal data and guarantee their access to the applicable Personal Data Processing Policy. Where processing requires authorization, they must verify that it has been given in a prior, express and informed manner. If the data subject does not authorize the processing or expresses their refusal, the Partner Organization must refrain from recording, entering or supplying their information through the Project Tools.

2Acceptance of the Terms and Conditions

By accepting these Terms and Conditions, the Partner Organization states that it has read and understood them and agrees to be bound by the obligations and responsibilities established here, including the Personal Data Processing Policy applicable to the Project and the other documents expressly incorporated into this Agreement.

Acceptance may be given through the electronic mechanisms provided by Caribe Afirmativo. The person who accepts on behalf of a Partner Organization declares that they have the capacity and authority necessary to represent and bind it in accordance with this Agreement.

The offer, acceptance and other expressions of will relating to these Terms and Conditions may be transmitted by means of equipment, systems, data messages or any other electronic medium that allows evidence of the acceptance and of the version of the Agreement accepted to be retained.

Paragraph. For all legal and contractual purposes, this Agreement shall be deemed concluded and signed in Barranquilla, Atlántico, Republic of Colombia, regardless of the place from which the Partner Organization expresses or transmits its acceptance.

3Obligations of Caribe Afirmativo

Under this Agreement, Caribe Afirmativo undertakes to:

  1. Guide and coordinate the activities necessary for the proper development of the Project and provide Partner Organizations with the guidelines and information necessary for their participation.
  2. Make available the tools and means necessary for carrying out the activities provided for in the Project, seeking to ensure that they function properly and adopting the reasonable security measures that apply.
  3. Inform Partner Organizations of the conditions applicable to the processing of personal data and make available to them the Personal Data Processing Policy, as well as the guidelines, procedures or documents they must observe during their participation.
  4. Process the information received in the course of the Project in accordance with the purposes notified to data subjects, the authorizations given where these are required and the applicable legal provisions.
  5. Adopt reasonable technical, human, administrative and organizational measures to preserve the confidentiality, integrity and security of the information managed under the Project.
  6. Handle, within the scope of its powers and responsibilities, the enquiries, requests or requirements relating to the development of the Project and cooperate with Partner Organizations where necessary for them to be properly addressed.
  7. Inform Partner Organizations in good time of any changes, instructions or circumstances that may substantially affect the activities they carry out under the Project.
  8. Comply with the other obligations corresponding to it under these Terms and Conditions and the law applicable to it.

4Obligations of Partner Organizations

Under this Agreement, Partner Organizations undertake to:

  1. Comply with these Terms and Conditions, the Personal Data Processing Policy applicable to the Project and the guidelines and instructions communicated by Caribe Afirmativo for carrying out the activities assigned to them.
  2. Facilitate participants' access to and voluntary provision of their information exclusively through the forms and Project Tools made available or expressly authorized by Caribe Afirmativo, providing them with clear and sufficient information about their purpose and about the processing of their personal data.
  3. Verify, where required, the data subject's prior, express and informed authorization for the processing of their personal data, and refrain from recording, entering or supplying information where the data subject does not give it or expresses their refusal.
  4. Use the information to which they have access exclusively for purposes related to the Project and refrain from using, reproducing, disclosing, transferring or making it available to third parties for purposes other than those authorized.
  5. Adopt the reasonable security and confidentiality measures necessary to protect the information to which they have access and ensure that their staff, collaborators, contractors or any person acting under their direction observes the obligations set out in this Agreement.
  6. Inform Caribe Afirmativo, without undue delay, of any loss, unauthorized access, disclosure, alteration, misuse, attack, breach or security incident of which they become aware and which may compromise information related to the Project.
  7. Respond in good time to the requests for information, documentation or cooperation made by Caribe Afirmativo in relation to compliance with this Agreement, the handling of data subjects' requests, security incidents or requirements of competent authorities.
  8. Comply with the legal and regulatory provisions applicable to the activities carried out by the Partner Organization in its respective jurisdiction and adopt the measures necessary to ensure that they are observed.
  9. Refrain from any action that may compromise the security of the Project Tools, from attempting to access information for which they are not authorized, or from giving their credentials, access or authentication mechanisms to unauthorized third parties.
  10. Inform Caribe Afirmativo in good time of any legal, technical or operational circumstance that may affect compliance with the obligations assumed under these Terms and Conditions.
  11. Comply with the other obligations corresponding to them under this Agreement and the applicable legal provisions.
  12. Refrain from taking, extracting, copying, downloading, reproducing or retaining for themselves, for third parties or for commercial or personal purposes, samples, copies or reproductions of software, code, databases, information, documents, content, methodologies and, in general, any work, development or asset of Caribe Afirmativo or made available for the development of the Project, unless there is prior express authorization or it is strictly necessary for carrying out the activities authorized in these Terms and Conditions.
  13. Cease their participation and withdraw from the Project when Caribe Afirmativo so requests, suspending the associated activities and access within the period it indicates. Caribe Afirmativo's decision to end a Partner Organization's participation shall not give rise to any right to indemnity, compensation or financial recognition in its favour, without prejudice to compliance with the obligations that, by their nature, must survive its withdrawal.
  14. Refrain from collecting, recording, storing, downloading, reproducing or retaining personal data and Project information outside the Project Tools made available or expressly authorized by Caribe Afirmativo, including physical files, spreadsheets, personal devices, email, messaging services, cloud storage services, external forms, applications, their own databases or any other unauthorized means. Exceptionally, where for technical or operational reasons it is essential to use a different medium temporarily, they must obtain the prior express authorization of Caribe Afirmativo, adopt the security measures it determines and delete the information from the temporary medium once it has been entered into the Project Tools.

5Nature of the relationship and autonomy

Participation in the Project is independent, collaborative and not-for-profit in nature. Acceptance of these Terms and Conditions does not create between Caribe Afirmativo and the Partner Organizations any employment relationship, partnership, mandate, agency, representation, association, joint venture or any other relationship other than the one expressly provided for in this Agreement.

The participation of Partner Organizations shall be directed exclusively to fulfilling the purposes and activities of the Project and may not be used to obtain economic or commercial benefits derived from the information, the personal data, the Project Tools or the participation of the beneficiaries.

Each Partner Organization shall retain its legal, administrative, technical and financial autonomy, shall act under its own responsibility and shall be responsible for complying with the legal and regulatory obligations applicable to it by reason of its nature, location and the activities it carries out.

Unless Caribe Afirmativo has given prior express authorization, no Partner Organization shall be entitled to act in its name, represent it, assume obligations on its behalf or make statements that may be understood as made on behalf of Caribe Afirmativo.

Paragraph. Participation in the Project does not give Partner Organizations any right to remuneration, financial consideration, profit sharing, commission or compensation from Caribe Afirmativo, unless expressly agreed otherwise in writing.

6Duration and term

This Agreement shall enter into force in respect of each Partner Organization from the moment it accepts these Terms and Conditions and shall remain in force for as long as it takes part in the Project or until one of the grounds for termination provided for in this Agreement arises.

The term of the Agreement shall not be conditional on the use of any specific technological tool and shall cover the activities carried out through the Project Tools that Caribe Afirmativo makes available, replaces, updates or implements during its performance.

Termination of the Agreement or the withdrawal of a Partner Organization shall not extinguish those obligations that, by their nature, must remain in force, particularly those relating to confidentiality, personal data protection, information security, intellectual property, liability and cooperation.

Paragraph. Caribe Afirmativo may terminate, suspend or end a Partner Organization's participation at any time, by communication through any means that allows a record of that decision to be kept, without this giving rise to any right to indemnity, compensation or financial recognition in favour of the Partner Organization.

7Confidentiality

Partner Organizations must maintain strict confidentiality in respect of the information to which they have access by reason of their participation in the Project, whatever its nature, format, medium or means of storage or transmission.

Information learned or received in the course of the Project may be used only to carry out the authorized activities and may not be revealed, disclosed, reproduced, published, delivered or made available to third parties, in whole or in part, except with the prior express authorization of Caribe Afirmativo or where there is a legal obligation or a requirement from a competent authority.

Partner Organizations must adopt the reasonable measures necessary to prevent unauthorized access to, use or disclosure of the information and ensure that their workers, collaborators, contractors, volunteers and other persons who, under their responsibility, have access to it assume and observe confidentiality duties equivalent to those set out in this Agreement.

Where a Partner Organization receives a request from an authority involving the delivery or disclosure of information related to the Project, it must inform Caribe Afirmativo in good time, where legally possible, and cooperate to seek a coordinated response, without prejudice to compliance with the legal duties applicable to it.

Paragraph. Confidentiality obligations shall survive the termination of the Agreement or the withdrawal of the Partner Organization from the Project, for as long as the information retains its confidential, restricted or protected character under the applicable law.

8Intellectual property

All studies, reports, documents, guides, methodologies, content, designs, materials, databases, compilations, technological developments, software, code and, in general, works or products eligible for protection by copyright or intellectual property rights that are created or developed specifically in the performance of the Project shall be the economic property of Caribe Afirmativo, on the terms permitted by the applicable law.

The participation of Partner Organizations in the Project's activities, including support to participants, facilitating access to the Project Tools, completing or assisting with the recording, collection or supply of information, as well as making observations, recommendations or technical or operational contributions, shall not by that fact alone confer on them the status of authors, co-authors or holders of economic copyright over the works, databases, studies, reports, results or other products derived from the Project.

To the extent that the contributions made by a Partner Organization may give rise in its favour to economic copyright or any other exploitation right capable of being disposed of, by accepting these Terms and Conditions the Partner Organization expressly waives, assigns and transfers to Caribe Afirmativo the economic rights that may lawfully be transferred, including, among others, the rights of reproduction, distribution, public communication, making available, transformation, adaptation, translation and other forms of exploitation permitted by the applicable law, in physical, digital or analogue media or through any technology now known or later devised, for all territories and for the maximum term of protection legally permitted.

Accordingly, Partner Organizations shall refrain from exploiting, commercializing, licensing, assigning, reproducing, distributing, transforming or authorizing third parties to exploit the works or products whose economic ownership corresponds to Caribe Afirmativo, except with its prior express authorization.

The foregoing does not cover the moral rights of authors which, under the applicable law, are inalienable, non-waivable or non-transferable, and which shall remain with their respective authors. Nor does it entail the transfer to Caribe Afirmativo of works, trademarks, methodologies, software or other intellectual property assets that previously belonged to a Partner Organization and were not created specifically in the course of the Project.

Partner Organizations warrant that any work, content or material they incorporate into the Project and over which they or third parties hold rights has the authorizations necessary for its use. They must also inform Caribe Afirmativo where they become aware of any unauthorized use, reproduction, appropriation or exploitation of the products or intellectual property assets linked to the Project and shall reasonably cooperate in the actions aimed at protecting them.

First paragraph. No co-authorship by participation

Participation in processes of gathering, recording, systematizing, validating, supplying or entering information, as well as the operational or technical support provided by a Partner Organization, does not in itself constitute an authorial contribution, nor does it give rise to any presumption of authorship, co-authorship, co-ownership or economic participation in the works, databases or other products resulting from the Project.

Second paragraph. Distinctive signs

Participation in the Project does not authorize Partner Organizations to use the name, trademarks, logos, distinctive signs or institutional identity of Caribe Afirmativo, except with prior express authorization and solely for the purposes and for the period it determines.

Third paragraph. Recognition of jurisdiction in intellectual property matters

Partner Organizations acknowledge that, for the purposes of disputes relating to copyright, related rights, industrial property, image rights and other intellectual property rights arising from the Project, the legal forum shall be that of the Republic of Colombia. Accordingly, and to the extent legally permitted, Partner Organizations waive the right to invoke any other forum or jurisdiction for the resolution of such disputes. For these purposes, the works, products, content and other developments generated in connection with the Project shall be deemed created, developed and delivered in the Republic of Colombia, regardless of the place from which the Partner Organization took part.

Fourth paragraph. Survival

The obligations and provisions established in this clause that, by their nature, must continue to have effect shall survive the withdrawal of the Partner Organization or the termination of this Agreement.

Fifth paragraph. Ownership and protection of the Project Tools

The Project Tools, as well as their content, interfaces, visual elements, graphics, designs, compilations, structures, functionalities, code, programs, software, applications, databases, documents and other elements eligible for protection by intellectual or industrial property rights (the “Project Materials”), are the property of Caribe Afirmativo or of the third parties that have authorized their use, as applicable, and are protected by Colombian law, the applicable international treaties and conventions and the other relevant intellectual and industrial property rules. The participation of Partner Organizations does not confer on them any ownership, licence or exploitation right over those Materials, beyond the limited use necessary for their participation in the Project. Accordingly, unless Caribe Afirmativo or the respective rights holder has given prior express authorization, Partner Organizations shall refrain from selling, licensing, sublicensing, distributing, copying, extracting, modifying, reproducing, transmitting, publishing, adapting, transforming, creating derivative works, reverse engineering, commercially exploiting or otherwise using the Project Materials for purposes other than those expressly authorized.

9Protection and processing of personal data

The Parties acknowledge that, in the course of the Project, activities of collection, recording, organization, storage, consultation, use, circulation, transmission, transfer, updating, deletion and, in general, processing of personal data may be carried out. These activities must be carried out in compliance with the principles of lawfulness, purpose limitation, freedom, transparency, restricted access and circulation, security, confidentiality and the other principles provided for in the applicable law.

Caribe Afirmativo shall determine the purposes and general conditions of the processing of the personal data collected under the Project and shall adopt the measures corresponding to it to guarantee compliance with the Personal Data Processing Policy and with applicable Colombian law.

Partner Organizations, when carrying out personal data processing activities on behalf of or following the instructions of Caribe Afirmativo, must act solely within the purposes, conditions and instructions established for the Project and may not use the data for their own, commercial or institutional purposes, or for those of third parties, other than those expressly authorized.

Partner Organizations must inform data subjects clearly, in advance and sufficiently about the processing of their personal data, guarantee their access to the Personal Data Processing Policy and, where legally required, verify that prior, express and informed authorization has been obtained. The data subject's refusal to authorize the processing must be respected and, in that event, the Partner Organization must refrain from entering, recording or supplying their data through the Project Tools.

Given that the Project may involve information relating to LGBTIQ+ people in contexts of human mobility and therefore sensitive personal data or information whose disclosure may create risks for data subjects, Partner Organizations must observe a reinforced duty of confidentiality, security, restricted access and use limited to the authorized purposes.

Each Partner Organization shall be responsible for knowing and complying with the mandatory personal data protection provisions applicable in the country or jurisdiction from which it carries out its activities, and must adopt the measures necessary for its participation in the Project to conform to those provisions. Where a local rule establishes an additional requirement or a higher level of protection, the Partner Organization must observe it and inform Caribe Afirmativo where it may affect the processing provided for in the Project.

Partner Organizations must cooperate with Caribe Afirmativo in handling enquiries, complaints and requests for access, updating, rectification, deletion, objection, withdrawal of authorization and the other rights that data subjects may exercise under the applicable law, forwarding in good time any request they receive relating to information processed under the Project.

First paragraph. Limitation of processing

Partner Organizations may not create parallel databases, retain copies, extract, download, re-use, cross-reference, profile, commercialize or use for their own purposes or those of third parties the personal data obtained or learned solely by reason of their participation in the Project, except on the express instruction or authorization of Caribe Afirmativo or where a legal obligation so requires.

Second paragraph. Applicable law

Without prejudice to this Agreement being governed by Colombian law, Partner Organizations must observe the mandatory personal data protection rules applicable in the jurisdictions in which they carry out their activities. Compliance with those provisions shall be the responsibility of each Partner Organization in respect of the activities it carries out in its own jurisdiction.

Third paragraph. Specific personal data protection obligations

The obligations set out in this clause constitute the general framework applicable to the processing of personal data under the Project and shall be supplemented by the specific personal data protection obligations, measures, procedures and safeguards established for Partner Organizations, having regard, among other factors, to their jurisdiction, the processing activities they carry out and the nature of the information involved.

Those specific provisions shall be incorporated into this Agreement and shall form an integral and binding part of these Terms and Conditions, so that compliance with them shall be mandatory for Partner Organizations to the extent they apply to them.

Where a special personal data protection provision applies to a particular Partner Organization, processing activity or jurisdiction, it shall prevail over the general provisions of this Agreement in respect of the matter specifically regulated, without prejudice to compliance with the other obligations established in these Terms and Conditions and in the Project's Personal Data Processing Policy.

Fourth paragraph. Processing solely through the Project Tools

The collection, recording, entry, consultation and other personal data processing activities that Partner Organizations carry out in the course of the Project must be performed exclusively through the Project Tools made available or expressly authorized by Caribe Afirmativo. Accordingly, Partner Organizations shall refrain from collecting, recording, storing, reproducing or retaining outside those tools copies of the personal data supplied for the Project, including their storage in physical files, spreadsheets, personal devices, email, messaging services, cloud storage services, external forms, applications or their own or third parties' databases.

Where, due to exceptional technical or operational circumstances, it is necessary to use a different medium temporarily, there must be prior express authorization from Caribe Afirmativo, the security measures it determines must be observed, and the information must be entered into the Project Tools and deleted from the temporary medium as soon as the circumstance that justified its use has ceased.

10Transfer and transmission of personal data

Participation in the Project may involve the national and international circulation, transfer or transmission of personal data, particularly where the information is supplied, consulted, stored or processed through the Project Tools from countries other than Colombia.

Partner Organizations acknowledge and accept that the personal data processed in the course of the Project may be received, stored and processed in the Republic of Colombia by Caribe Afirmativo, in accordance with the purposes notified to data subjects, the Personal Data Processing Policy, the authorizations given where these are required and the other applicable legal bases.

Each Partner Organization must verify, before carrying out or facilitating an international transfer or transmission of personal data, that the operation is permitted under the law applicable in its own jurisdiction and that the corresponding authorizations, information duties, safeguards, contractual clauses, assessments, registrations, communications or other requirements have been met.

Where the law of the country from which the data are sent establishes special requirements for their international transfer or transmission, the Partner Organization must comply with them and adopt the measures necessary to legitimize the circulation of the information to Colombia, including, where appropriate, signing or acceding to contractual clauses, instruments or additional safeguards incorporated into this Agreement.

Partner Organizations may not transfer, transmit, disclose, allow access to or make available to third parties the personal data processed under the Project, within or outside their own jurisdiction, for purposes other than those authorized or without the prior authorization of Caribe Afirmativo, unless there is a legal obligation or a requirement from a competent authority.

Participation in the Project does not authorize Partner Organizations to change on their own the destination, the recipients, the purposes or the conditions under which the international circulation of personal data takes place.

First paragraph. Additional safeguards

Where the applicable law requires model contractual clauses, standard clauses, transfer agreements, adequacy safeguards or other specific mechanisms to permit the international circulation of personal data, these must be observed by the Partner Organization and may be incorporated into this Agreement as special provisions, forming an integral and binding part of it.

Second paragraph. Responsibility of the Partner Organization

Each Partner Organization shall be responsible for complying with the legal requirements enforceable in the jurisdiction from which it collects, records, supplies, transfers or transmits the personal data, and must refrain from carrying out the operation where it finds that this would breach an applicable mandatory provision. In that case, it must inform Caribe Afirmativo in good time so that the measures necessary for it to continue participating in the Project can be determined.

Third paragraph. Interpretation favourable to the protection of the data subject

Where provisions of different jurisdictions apply to the same international transfer or transmission operation, the Parties must observe the applicable mandatory rules and seek to adopt the safeguards that ensure an adequate level of protection of data subjects' rights.

11Information security

Partner Organizations must adopt and maintain reasonable and appropriate technical, human, administrative and organizational measures to protect the information and personal data processed under the Project against loss, destruction, alteration, misuse, disclosure, access, consultation, extraction, copying or unauthorized or fraudulent processing. In particular, Partner Organizations must:

  1. Limit access to the information and to the Project Tools exclusively to the persons who require it to carry out the authorized activities.
  2. Adopt reasonable measures for the proper management and safekeeping of usernames, passwords, credentials and other authentication mechanisms, which shall be personal and may not be shared with unauthorized third parties.
  3. Refrain from carrying out or allowing actions aimed at breaching, circumventing, altering or affecting the security mechanisms of the Project Tools, including unauthorized access attempts, hacking, cracking, unauthorized reverse engineering, the introduction of malicious code or malware, or any action aimed at compromising their security, availability or integrity.
  4. Adopt reasonable preventive measures against cyberattacks, phishing, malware, impersonation, mass extraction of information and other threats that may compromise the personal data or the Project's information.
  5. Avoid storing, downloading, reproducing or retaining Project information on devices, accounts, cloud services, applications or media not authorized by Caribe Afirmativo.
  6. Ensure that the persons acting under their authority or responsibility who have access to Project information are aware of and comply with the applicable security and confidentiality measures.
  7. Immediately report to Caribe Afirmativo any loss of devices, compromise of credentials, suspicious access, vulnerability or circumstance that may compromise the confidentiality, integrity, availability or security of the information.
  8. Follow the instructions, recommendations or corrective measures communicated by Caribe Afirmativo where these are necessary to prevent, contain, mitigate or correct risks relating to information security.

First paragraph. Prohibition on circumventing security controls

Partner Organizations may not deactivate, modify, evade or interfere with the access, authentication, traceability, security or any other protection mechanism implemented in the Project Tools.

Second paragraph. Responsibility for access

Each Partner Organization shall be responsible for properly managing the access granted to the persons acting under its authority and must request or carry out, as applicable, its immediate suspension or removal when those persons cease to be authorized to take part in the Project. They must also inform them about the Project's Personal Data Processing Policy and the importance of the processing of LGBTIQ+ people's data.

Third paragraph. Scope

The obligations set out in this clause shall be enforceable regardless of the technology, platform, application, infrastructure or tool used at any given time for the development of the Project.

12Security incidents and breaches

Partner Organizations must inform Caribe Afirmativo, without undue delay and as soon as they become aware, of any actual or reasonably suspected incident that may compromise the confidentiality, integrity, availability or security of the personal data or of the information processed under the Project.

For the purposes of this clause, security incidents shall include, among others, the loss, destruction, alteration, disclosure, extraction, access or unauthorized processing of information; the compromise or loss of credentials or devices; cyberattacks, hacking, cracking, phishing, malware, impersonation, system breaches, mass extraction of information and any other event that may compromise the personal data, the Project Tools or the information related to it.

The Partner Organization must immediately adopt the reasonable measures available to it to contain, mitigate and prevent the spread of the incident, preserve the available information and evidence, and refrain from any action that may hinder its investigation or management.

The communication to Caribe Afirmativo must contain, to the extent the information is available, a description of the incident, the date and circumstances in which it was detected, the nature and categories of the information compromised, the data subjects potentially affected, the possible consequences, the measures adopted or proposed and any other information reasonably necessary for its assessment and handling. The initial absence of any of these elements shall not justify delaying the communication of the incident.

Where the incident must be reported to an administrative, judicial, data protection or other authority, or requires communication to data subjects, the Partner Organization must inform Caribe Afirmativo beforehand and seek a coordinated response, provided this is legally possible and does not entail failing to meet a deadline or legal obligation applicable in its jurisdiction.

Where the law requires immediate communication or communication within a period that does not allow for prior coordination, the Partner Organization must comply with that obligation in good time and inform Caribe Afirmativo as soon as possible, providing it with a copy of the communication, request, response or corresponding documentation, where it may legally do so.

Partner Organizations must respond in good time to the requests for information, documentation, clarification or cooperation made by Caribe Afirmativo in connection with an incident and cooperate during its investigation, containment, mitigation, documentation, response and closure, including the steps to be taken before authorities or data subjects.

First paragraph. Duty of confidentiality

Partner Organizations shall refrain from publicly disclosing information relating to a security incident, unless there is a legal obligation, a requirement from a competent authority or the prior authorization of Caribe Afirmativo.

Second paragraph. Recording and retention of evidence

Partner Organizations must retain, in accordance with the applicable law and for as long as is reasonably necessary, the records, communications, evidence and other documentation relating to the incident and make them available to Caribe Afirmativo when it requires them for its investigation, documentation or handling.

Third paragraph. Liability

Caribe Afirmativo's coordination of or involvement in the handling of an incident shall not transfer or exclude the liability corresponding to the Partner Organization for the facts, acts or omissions attributable to it under this Agreement and the applicable law.

13Cooperation and handling of requests

Partner Organizations must provide Caribe Afirmativo with the cooperation reasonably necessary for the proper fulfilment of the obligations arising from the Project and the applicable law, particularly in matters relating to personal data protection, information security, the exercise of data subjects' rights and requirements of competent authorities. In particular, Partner Organizations must:

  1. Respond fully, truthfully and in good time to the requests for information, documentation, supporting evidence or clarification made by Caribe Afirmativo in relation to the activities carried out under the Project.
  2. Inform Caribe Afirmativo, as soon as legally possible, of any requirement, investigation, administrative or judicial proceeding, claim, complaint or request from an authority or a data subject that relates to the Project or to information processed in the course of it.
  3. Where legally possible, refrain from issuing a final response, providing explanations or supplying information related to the Project to an authority without having informed Caribe Afirmativo beforehand, so that a coordinated and consistent response between the Parties can be sought.
  4. Where the applicable law imposes a deadline that does not allow for prior coordination, respond to the requirement in good time and inform Caribe Afirmativo as soon as possible, forwarding a copy of the request, the response and the corresponding documentation, provided it may legally do so.
  5. Cooperate in preparing responses, reports, explanations, corrective actions, improvement plans and other measures necessary to address requirements of authorities or claims by data subjects.
  6. Provide the information and documentation reasonably necessary for Caribe Afirmativo to verify compliance with the obligations assumed by the Partner Organization under these Terms and Conditions.
  7. Retain, for the periods required by the applicable law or for as long as necessary to demonstrate compliance with its obligations, the supporting documents and records relating to the activities carried out under the Project.

First paragraph. Coordinated action

Whenever legally possible, Caribe Afirmativo and the Partner Organization shall seek to coordinate the steps taken in relation to requirements of authorities, investigations, claims or the exercise of data subjects' rights. This coordination shall not mean that Caribe Afirmativo assumes the obligations or responsibilities that legally or contractually correspond to the Partner Organization.

Second paragraph. Continuing duty of cooperation

The cooperation obligations set out in this clause shall survive the withdrawal of the Partner Organization or the termination of the Agreement in respect of facts, processing operations, incidents or actions that occurred during its participation in the Project.

14Liability and warranties

Each Party shall be responsible for complying with the obligations corresponding to it under these Terms and Conditions, as well as for the acts or omissions directly attributable to it in the course of the Project.

Partner Organizations shall be liable for the damages, claims or consequences arising from the failure to comply with the obligations assumed under this Agreement, particularly where these originate in the unauthorized use of the information, the failure to follow the instructions issued by Caribe Afirmativo, the breach of confidentiality or security duties, the improper processing of personal data or the failure to comply with the legal provisions applicable to them in their own jurisdiction.

Partner Organizations declare and warrant that they will carry out the activities assigned to them diligently, in good faith and in compliance with these Terms and Conditions, the Personal Data Processing Policy, the specific provisions incorporated into the Agreement and the rules applicable in their own jurisdiction.

The guidance, assistance, coordination or cooperation that Caribe Afirmativo provides in relation to a claim, incident, investigation or requirement from an authority shall not entail the assumption, substitution or transfer of the liability corresponding to the Partner Organization for its own acts or omissions.

Where, as a result of an act or omission attributable to a Partner Organization, Caribe Afirmativo becomes the subject of a claim, investigation, administrative or judicial proceeding, penalty or third-party action, the Partner Organization must provide all the cooperation necessary to address it and answer for the damages legally attributable to it, in accordance with the applicable law.

First paragraph. No automatic penalty

Second paragraph. Independence of liability

Joint participation in the Project does not entail joint and several liability, general shared responsibility or the automatic assumption by Caribe Afirmativo of the obligations or responsibilities attributable to Partner Organizations, unless a mandatory legal provision establishes otherwise.

15Termination

This Agreement and a Partner Organization's participation in the Project may end on any of the following grounds:

  1. By decision of Caribe Afirmativo, communicated to the Partner Organization by any means that allows a record of it to be kept, without the need to invoke a specific ground and without this giving rise to any right to indemnity, compensation or financial recognition.
  2. By decision of the Partner Organization to withdraw voluntarily from the Project, upon prior communication to Caribe Afirmativo.
  3. For total or partial failure to comply with any of the obligations established in these Terms and Conditions, the Personal Data Processing Policy or the specific provisions forming an integral part of the Agreement.
  4. For the unauthorized use, access, extraction, retention, disclosure, transfer, transmission or processing of personal data or information related to the Project.
  5. For failure to comply with the confidentiality, information security, intellectual property or cooperation obligations set out in this Agreement.
  6. For carrying out actions that may compromise the security, integrity, availability or operation of the Project Tools or put at risk the rights of the data subjects.
  7. For failure to comply with the law applicable to the Partner Organization where this may affect the development of the Project, Caribe Afirmativo, the data subjects or third parties.
  8. Where the continued participation of the Partner Organization may reasonably represent a legal, reputational, operational, technological or security risk for Caribe Afirmativo, the Project, the participants or the information processed.
  9. For the termination, definitive suspension or impossibility of continuing with the Project.
  10. By mutual agreement between Caribe Afirmativo and the Partner Organization.
  11. On the other grounds provided for in these Terms and Conditions or in the applicable law.

First paragraph. Precautionary suspension

Where Caribe Afirmativo identifies a risk to the security of the information, the personal data, the participants or the proper development of the Project, it may immediately and as a precaution suspend the Partner Organization's access or activities while the relevant circumstances are assessed.

Second paragraph. Effectiveness

Where the nature or seriousness of the situation so justifies, particularly in the face of risks relating to personal data, confidentiality or information security, Caribe Afirmativo may order its immediate application.

Third paragraph. Outstanding obligations

16Effects of termination

Once this Agreement has been terminated or a Partner Organization's participation in the Project has ended for any reason, that organization must immediately cease the activities it was carrying out under it and its access to or use of the Project Tools, unless Caribe Afirmativo expressly authorizes otherwise. From termination, the Partner Organization must:

  1. Cease any activity of collection, recording, consultation, use, circulation, transmission, transfer or any other form of processing of personal data carried out on behalf of or under the Project.
  2. Refrain from accessing or attempting to access the Project Tools and stop using the credentials, usernames, authentication mechanisms or permissions assigned to it.
  3. Deliver to Caribe Afirmativo, where applicable and upon its request, the information, documentation, supporting documents, records and other items relating to the activities carried out under the Project that are in its custody or under its control.
  4. Securely delete the copies of personal data, databases, documents and Project information held on its devices, systems, accounts, storage services or any other medium, unless a legal obligation requires their retention.
  5. Where there is a legal retention obligation, limit the processing of the information exclusively to that purpose, maintain the corresponding security and confidentiality measures and delete it once the reason justifying its retention has ceased.
  6. Return, delete or stop using the documents, materials, software, content, developments and other intellectual property assets of Caribe Afirmativo or of third parties to which it had access in connection with the Project.
  7. Adopt the measures necessary to ensure that its workers, collaborators, contractors, volunteers and other persons who had access to the Project Tools or to its information likewise cease any access or processing.
  8. Continue to provide the cooperation necessary in respect of data subjects' requests, security incidents, investigations, requirements of authorities, claims or proceedings relating to facts that occurred during its participation in the Project.

First paragraph. Evidence of compliance

Caribe Afirmativo may ask the Partner Organization to certify or demonstrate compliance with the obligations of return, deletion, restriction of processing and closure of access set out in this clause, for which purpose the organization must supply the information or supporting documents reasonably required.

Second paragraph. The Partner Organization's own information

Termination shall not require the Partner Organization to delete information it obtained lawfully and independently of the Project, provided it has its own legal basis for processing it and its retention does not derive from the access granted by Caribe Afirmativo. In no case may it continue to use information obtained solely in connection with the Project by relying on this paragraph.

Third paragraph. Survival

Termination shall not extinguish the obligations relating to confidentiality, personal data protection, intellectual property, liability, information security and cooperation, which shall remain enforceable for the period corresponding to their nature and to the applicable law.

Partner Organizations may not assign, transfer or delegate, in whole or in part, their participation, contractual position, obligations, access or powers arising from this Agreement to another natural or legal person without the prior express authorization of Caribe Afirmativo.

Caribe Afirmativo's authorization for the involvement of a third party shall not release the Partner Organization from the obligations and responsibilities assumed under this Agreement, nor shall it imply the existence of a direct contractual relationship between Caribe Afirmativo and that third party, unless expressly agreed otherwise.

A Partner Organization's participation from a jurisdiction other than Colombia shall not in itself change the law applicable to this Agreement or its place of execution, without prejudice to each Partner Organization's duty to know and comply with the mandatory rules applicable to it in its own jurisdiction, particularly those relating to personal data protection, privacy, information security and the other matters governed by these Terms and Conditions.

Paragraph. Where there is a conflict between a provision of this Agreement and a mandatory rule applicable to a Partner Organization in its own jurisdiction, that organization must inform Caribe Afirmativo in good time and refrain from carrying out the affected activity until the measures necessary to ensure compliance have been determined, without this in itself entailing a change to the law applicable to the Agreement.

21Dispute resolution

Differences and disputes concerning the interpretation, performance, compliance, non-compliance or termination of the obligations arising from this Agreement shall first be addressed through direct settlement between Caribe Afirmativo and the Partner Organization concerned.

If no agreement is reached, the Parties must then turn to a duly authorized conciliation centre in the city of Barranquilla, Atlántico, Republic of Colombia, in order to seek a negotiated solution to the dispute.

If the dispute is not resolved through conciliation, it shall be submitted to an Arbitration Tribunal seated in the city of Barranquilla, Atlántico, Republic of Colombia, which shall decide according to law, in accordance with Colombian legislation and the rules of the arbitration and conciliation centre before which the proceedings are conducted.

Paragraph. The provisions of this clause shall not prevent either Party from applying to the competent judicial or administrative authority for precautionary, interim or urgent measures where these are necessary to protect personal data, confidential information or intellectual property rights, or to prevent imminent harm.
